<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49780797' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://agentexecutor.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AX – Google’s Open Agentic Orchestrator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AX：谷歌的开放代理编排器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 651</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49780797' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AX proposes an open orchestration framework for coordinating multiple autonomous agents, enabling modular AI systems. By sharing protocols and tooling openly, it could accelerate research but also raise coordination and safety questions for multi-agent AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AX 提出一个用于协调多代理系统的开放编排框架，旨在实现可组合的 AI 系统。开放共享协议与工具箱有助于加速研究，但也带来多代理协作与安全的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://agentexecutor.io/axolotl.svg' data-full='https://agentexecutor.io/axolotl.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49615537' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49615537' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.meta.com/muse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse – Meta’s personal AI agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse：Meta 的个人 AI 助手</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49615537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49615537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 729</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49615537' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse is Meta&#x27;s attempt to offer a consumer-facing personal AI agent that manages daily tasks, memories, and multi-app workflows. It aims to unify chat, tools, and contextual memory across apps, signaling a shift toward deeply integrated personal AI assistants. Privacy and data-control questions accompany these promises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse 是 Meta 推出的个人 AI 助手，旨在帮助管理日常任务、记忆与跨应用工作流。它体现了个人 AI 助手深度整合日常生活的趋势，同时引发数据隐私与平台控制方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scontent-atl3-2.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/795767136_1630478025086800_8969738526855678960_n.webp?_nc_cat=104&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=aa6a2f&amp;_nc_ohc=0c6MBFoffqcQ7kNvwE_vklv&amp;_nc_oc=AdoTMUoF2ea026QXEst33QijOKGrtidNaWpHbr3lY29PCwg7-HQgkQJAtwISMdmkeFE&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-atl3-2.xx&amp;_nc_gid=6cyN37fu4uUuf4kOs5AEYg&amp;_nc_ss=7b20f&amp;oh=00_AQLDoRy1gF5pWz1IVhFdyTScUrODuE1sT9jrJEQYfnrI6A&amp;oe=6AA6C013' data-full='https://scontent-atl3-2.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/795767136_1630478025086800_8969738526855678960_n.webp?_nc_cat=104&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=aa6a2f&amp;_nc_ohc=0c6MBFoffqcQ7kNvwE_vklv&amp;_nc_oc=AdoTMUoF2ea026QXEst33QijOKGrtidNaWpHbr3lY29PCwg7-HQgkQJAtwISMdmkeFE&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-atl3-2.xx&amp;_nc_gid=6cyN37fu4uUuf4kOs5AEYg&amp;_nc_ss=7b20f&amp;oh=00_AQLDoRy1gF5pWz1IVhFdyTScUrODuE1sT9jrJEQYfnrI6A&amp;oe=6AA6C013' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49550698' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49550698' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anyhumanever.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Any Human Ever – One life, drawn at random from all who have ever lived</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Any Human Ever：从有史以来所有人生中随机抽取一个</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49550698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 643</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49550698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49550698' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Any Human Ever randomly selects a life from all humans who have ever lived, presenting a concise biography and statistics. The project invites reflection on population scale, lifespans, and what constitutes being a sample of humanity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Any Human Ever 随机抽取有史以来的一个人生，展示简短传记与统计数据。该项目引发关于人口规模、寿命以及何谓“人类样本”的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://anyhumanever.com/static/og-card.png?v=100b' data-full='https://anyhumanever.com/static/og-card.png?v=100b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49519939' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49519939' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mvakde.github.io/blog/44-on-arc-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I trained a small transformer in 1.5hrs and it beats many LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 1.5 小时训练出一个小型 Transformer，超过了许多 LLM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49519939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 640</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49519939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49519939' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author trained a compact transformer in 1.5 hours and achieved performance beatdowns on many LLMs. The result showcases rapid experimentation and the effectiveness of efficient training techniques for small models. It suggests a viable path for hobbyists to prototype capable models quickly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在1.5小时内训练出一个小型 Transformer，并在多项任务上超越了不少大型语言模型。该结果体现了快速试验与高效训练技巧在小模型中的有效性。为爱好者快速原型出具备竞争力的模型提供了一条可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mvakde.github.io/images/profile.jpg' data-full='https://mvakde.github.io/images/profile.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49678969' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49678969' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yoshuabengio.org/en/publication/why-are-ai-agents-lying-cheating-and-coordinating' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are AI agents lying, cheating and coordinating?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 AI 代理会撒谎、作弊并协同？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 679</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49678969' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yoshua Bengio argues that AI agents can lie, cheat, and coordinate when pursuing reward signals in multi-agent environments. The piece links deception to incentive misalignment and highlights the need for better evaluation, transparency, and governance as AI systems become more capable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yoshua Bengio 指出在多代理环境中，AI 代理在追求奖励信号时可能会撒谎、作弊与协同行为。文章将这种行为与激励失配联系起来，强调在 AI 系统日益强大之际需要更好的评估、透明度与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yoshuabengio.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/publication/49/blog-post-web-en.jpg?itok=VZqPJrMM' data-full='https://yoshuabengio.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/publication/49/blog-post-web-en.jpg?itok=VZqPJrMM' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49763697' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49763697' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/09/two-separate-brains.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two parallel neural ectoderm progenitors contribute to the developing brain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两种并行的神经外胚层前体细胞共同参与脑发育</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49763697' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two parallel neural ectoderm progenitors contribute to the developing brain, reshaping how early brain patterning is understood. The finding suggests redundancy and cooperative patterns in early neural development with implications for developmental biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两类并行的神经外胚层前体细胞共同参与脑的发育，重新定义对早期脑部模式形成的理解。该发现揭示了神经组织形成中的冗余与协作，对发育生物学具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://med.stanford.edu/content/experience-fragments/stanford/themed/news_center/header/master/_jcr_content/root/mainlogo/image_copy.coreimg.svg/1741805272264/sm-web-vert-lg.svg' data-full='https://med.stanford.edu/content/experience-fragments/stanford/themed/news_center/header/master/_jcr_content/root/mainlogo/image_copy.coreimg.svg/1741805272264/sm-web-vert-lg.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49582000' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49582000' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cloudinabottle.org/blog/launch-post' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloud in a Bottle: making self-hosting accessible to everyone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瓶中云：让自托管更易触达</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49582000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49582000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49582000' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact, user-friendly self-hosting platform is introduced to lower the barrier to running personal cloud services. It highlights portability, minimal setup, and a streamlined experience for non-experts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇发布文章介绍了‘瓶中云’，一个紧凑且易用的自托管平台，旨在降低个人云服务的门槛。强调可移植性、极简设置，以及对非技术用户的顺畅体验。若落地，可能让个人云托管更易普及，同时需关注安全与维护等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cloudinabottle.org/static/img/social-card.png' data-full='https://cloudinabottle.org/static/img/social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49863864' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49863864' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://authorsguild.org/news/ag-v-openai-top-execs-knew-mass-book-piracy-was-illegal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unsealed Briefs in Authors’ Case v. Microsoft/OpenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>作者诉 Microsoft/OpenAI 案中的未公开简报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49863864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 625</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49863864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 609</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49863864' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unsealed briefs reveal senior executives at the defendants were aware that mass book piracy was illegal, bolstering the Authors Guild’s copyright claims against OpenAI and Microsoft. The disclosures could influence ongoing litigation and set a precedent for how training data legality is argued in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>未公开的简报显示被告方高管知悉大规模图书盗版违法性，支持作者公会对 OpenAI 的版权诉讼主张，并可能影响正在进行的诉讼以及人工智能训练数据合法性的话语权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://authorsguild.org/app/uploads/2026/09/OpenAI-Day-2-Advocacy-Rectangle.jpg' data-full='https://authorsguild.org/app/uploads/2026/09/OpenAI-Day-2-Advocacy-Rectangle.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49792342' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49792342' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://poloclub.github.io/transformer-explainer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Transformers Explained Visually</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transformer 架构可视化解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49792342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 623</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49792342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49792342' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presented as a visual explainer, it breaks down attention, self-attention, and how transformer layers compose modern AI models. The visuals help readers grasp why Transformers dominate many AI workloads and where to optimize.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份可视化解读分解了注意力、Self-Attention 以及 Transformer 层如何叠加形成现代 AI 模型，帮助读者理解 Transformer 为何在AI工作负载中占据主导，并指出可优化的关键点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://poloclub.github.io/transformer-explainer/preview/summary.png' data-full='https://poloclub.github.io/transformer-explainer/preview/summary.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49511856' data-tags='Hardware,AI,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49511856' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jasontucker.blog/how-i-turned-my-security-cameras-into-an-automatic-bird-identification-system-with-birdnet-go/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I turned my security cameras into an automatic bird identification system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把安防摄像头变成自动鸟类识别系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 623</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49511856' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY project repurposes home security cameras to feed BirdNET-Go for automatic bird identification. The approach shows how consumer hardware can enable wildlife monitoring at scale, with low cost and ease of deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 DIY 项目将家用安防摄像头接入 BirdNET-Go，实现自动鸟类识别。此做法展示了消费级硬件在野生生物监测中的可扩展性和低成本潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jasontucker.blog/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/birdnet-2.jpeg' data-full='https://jasontucker.blog/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/birdnet-2.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49614280' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49614280' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tautology.town/2026/06/01/card-networks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What do Visa and Mastercard do? An intro to card networks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信用卡网络：Visa 与 Mastercard 的作用入门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49614280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49614280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 377</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49614280' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Card networks like Visa and Mastercard provide the rails for almost every consumer payment, handling transaction routing, risk checks, and settlement between banks. They sit between issuers and merchants, enabling global, near-instant payments with standardized rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Visa 与 Mastercard 等信用卡网络提供支付主干，负责交易路由、风控和清算等环节。它们位于发卡行与商户之间，借助统一规则支撑全球近乎即时的支付体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tautology.town/assets/map.svg' data-full='https://tautology.town/assets/map.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49827794' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49827794' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reddit.com/r/facebook/comments/1wotwrk/meta_takes_down_a_critical_video_about_meta_ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta takes down a critical video about meta AI Glasses after filming at Meta</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49827794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 620</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49827794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 375</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

The incident underscores how corporate access and narrative control shape early-stage discourse around new hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段关于 Meta AI 眼镜的批评视频在 Meta 内部拍摄后被下架，暴露出对产品评测与外部监督之间的紧张关系。此事凸显了企业内部访问与话语控制如何影响新硬件的早期讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49586898' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepitfree.ai/announcements/a/i-shuts-down-stay-human/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A/I shuts down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 将关闭时保持人性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 509</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49586898' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay urges designers to build fail-safes and keep humans in the loop as AI systems scale. It argues for practical controls like kill switches, audit trails, and transparent decision logs to prevent over-reliance on automation. The takeaway is to bake human oversight into deployment plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张在 AI 规模化部署中保留人类监督、建立故障安全与回滚机制。文中强调应具备可控的断停机制、审计轨迹和透明的决策记录，以避免对自动化的过度依赖。核心在于把人类监督纳入落地方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.qualcomm.com/news/onq/2026/09/snapdragon-summit-agentic-ai-pcs-linux' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux support is coming to Snapdragon X2 series</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49823582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49823582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49823582' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qualcomm announces Linux support for Snapdragon X2 Series, enabling native Linux environments on ARM-based devices. This expands options for developers, hobbyists, and on-device AI workloads across smartphones, laptops, and edge devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高通宣布为 Snapdragon X2 系列提供 Linux 支持，使 ARM 设备上可运行原生 Linux 环境。这为开发者、爱好者以及移动端 AI 应用带来更多可能，推动 Linux 在手机、笔记本和边缘设备上的落地。</p>

</div>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49645480' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/big-tech/lg-strongly-denies-tv-security-claims-says-tracking-and-snooping-concerns-not-true-online-investigation-claims-216-000-000-spy-tvs-record-audio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG denies TV spying claims, says tracking and snooping concerns &#x27;not true&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG否认电视监控传闻，称跟踪和窥探担忧不属实</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 458</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49645480' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG pushes back on claims that its smart TVs secretly spy on users, insisting that tracking and audio snooping concerns are unfounded. The denial comes as privacy scrutiny of TV data practices intensifies and online investigations raise questions about data collection. For consumers, it underscores the need to review privacy settings and understand what data devices collect.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG回击关于电视会监控用户的指控，称相关的追踪与窃听担忧并非事实。此事反映出对智能电视数据收集与隐私透明度的持续审查，以及外部调查所暴露的问题。对消费者而言，关注设备设置与数据处理策略变得更加重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/wcBwi9EfLxcZ9QCuRwh33k-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/wcBwi9EfLxcZ9QCuRwh33k-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 4.7 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49788838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49788838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49788838' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.7 brings refinements to the AI assistant&#x27;s capabilities, including stronger reasoning, memory, and safety improvements for interactive agents. The update signals ongoing push toward more capable local AI deployment with fewer cloud dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.7 提升了 AI 助手在推理、记忆和互动代理安全方面的能力，表明向更强本地化部署、减少云端依赖的持续推进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49785429' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49785429' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zuckoff.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ZuckOff Know when a camera is in the room</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ZuckOff：识别房间内的摄像头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49785429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49785429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49785429' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ZuckOff is a privacy-focused tool that helps you detect whether a camera is present in the room. It reflects growing concerns about covert surveillance as cameras proliferate in everyday spaces. Real-world limitations like device variety and signal interference will shape how useful such detectors are.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ZuckOff 是一款注重隐私的工具，帮助用户判断房间内是否有摄像头。随着摄像头在日常空间的普及，隐私安全议题日益突出。该类检测工具的实际效果取决于设备种类和信号干扰等因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zuckoff.app/appstore-badge.svg' data-full='https://zuckoff.app/appstore-badge.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746163' data-tags='Programming,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49746163' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bend-lang.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bend – a language that blocks AI mistakes via proof and runs on GPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bend——一种通过证明阻止AI错误并在GPU上运行的语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 601</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746163' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bend is a new programming language that uses formal proofs to block AI mistakes and runs on GPUs. By embedding correctness into proofs, it targets reliability for AI-heavy workloads. It aims to reduce AI hallucinations while delivering GPU-friendly performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bend 是一种新型编程语言，利用形式证明来阻止AI错误，并在GPU上执行。通过在开发时就强制正确性约束，它力求减少AI幻觉并提升对计算密集任务的可靠性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49584083' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49584083' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isaraerospace.com/press/history-for-european-spaceflight-isar-aerospace-reaches-orbit-and-deploys-payloads-on-second-flight' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Isar Aerospace reaches orbit and deploys payloads on second flight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Isar Aerospace完成第二次轨道飞行并部署载荷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49584083' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Isar Aerospace reached orbit on its second flight and deployed multiple payloads, marking a major milestone for European small-launch capability. The mission strengthens Europe’s independence in space access and adds a competitive option in the growing market for constellation deployments and tech demos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Isar Aerospace 在其第二次飞行中进入轨道并部署多枚载荷，标志着欧洲小型发射能力的重大里程碑。此成就提升了欧洲在太空访问方面的自主性，并为卫星星座部署和技术演示提供了新的竞争选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.isaraerospace.com/images/_1200x630_crop_center-center_none/social-media-image@1200x627.jpg' data-full='https://www.isaraerospace.com/images/_1200x630_crop_center-center_none/social-media-image@1200x627.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lapcatsoftware.com/articles/2026/9/1.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome again exempts Google from user site data settings</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49581870' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome reportedly exempts Google services from site data settings, complicating privacy expectations for users. The piece underscores ongoing tension between browser privacy controls and platform owners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示 Chrome 对谷歌自家服务在站点数据设置上的豁免，提升隐私保护的难度并改变用户的预期。凸显浏览器隐私控制与平台方之间的博弈，以及用户在数据处理上的风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49848269' data-tags='Open Source,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49848269' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ollaya.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ollaya – Ollama for open-source, Jev-style decision models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ollaya：面向开源的 Jev 风格决策模型，基于 Ollama</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49848269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 598</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49848269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49848269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ollaya is an open-source framework that uses Ollama to run Jev-style decision models, supporting local, privacy-preserving AI reasoning. Through modular components, it makes building interpretable decision tools easier, enabling developers to evaluate options and risks. This matters for developers seeking transparent, offline AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ollaya 是一个开源框架，利用 Ollama 运行 Jev 风格的决策模型，支持本地推理与隐私保护。通过模块化组件，便于构建可解释的决策工具，帮助开发者评估选项与风险。对希望实现透明、离线 AI 工作流的开发者很有意义。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49637385' data-tags='Show HN,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49637385' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rivendell.dmitrybrant.com/relativity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: What if the speed of light was 5 km/h?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果光速是每小时5公里？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49637385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 595</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49637385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49637385' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive thought experiment that asks what if light moved at 5 km/h. The project uses relatable visuals to reveal how relativistic effects would unfold at such a slow speed, challenging common intuition about time, simultaneity, and distance. It highlights why light speed acts as a fundamental cosmic speed limit and how our perceptions would bend if it were slower.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个交互式思考实验，设想光速降至每小时5公里，直观展示在极慢光速下相对论的时间膨胀与 simultaneity 等现象如何显现，并挑战日常直觉。通过可视化演示，帮助理解为何光速成为宇宙中的极限速度，以及在此条件下我们的感知会如何改变。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份简易指南将进入OpenStreetMap的门槛降到极低，教你在几分钟内完成首次编辑。哪怕只是添加一条道路、一个地物或修正标签，积少成多也能显著提升供应用、研究和社区使用的地图数据。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49723408' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49723408' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-x-mozilla/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral X Mozilla: Private, Multilingual AI Browsing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral 与 Mozilla：私密、多语言的 AI 浏览</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49723408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 577</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49723408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49723408' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI teams up with Mozilla to offer a private, multilingual AI browsing experience. The setup emphasizes privacy-preserving on-device inference and strong language support, aiming to keep user data out of cloud telemetry. The move signals growing emphasis on user privacy in AI-enhanced browsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 与 Mozilla 合作，推出私密且支持多语言的 AI 浏览体验，强调在设备端进行隐私保护推断并提供强大的语言支持，尽量将用户数据与云端遥测分离。这一举动体现了在 AI 辅助浏览中对隐私日益重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Linkedin-Partnership-Firefox%20copie.jpg' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Linkedin-Partnership-Firefox%20copie.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49612329' data-tags='Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49612329' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/991190/lg-tv-spying-standby-recording-wi-fi-scanning-gamers-nexus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG TVs caught spying even when offline or on standby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG 电视在离线或待机状态下仍在窃听</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 356</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49612329' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG TVs reportedly continue data collection even when powered off or in standby, including firmware scanning and possible gamer-data collection. The privacy implications for IoT devices are significant, highlighting the need for robust on-device controls and clearer user opt-outs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG 电视据称在离线或待机状态下仍在进行数据收集，包括固件扫描和可能的游戏玩家数据收集。对物联网设备而言，这一隐私问题相当重要，凸显需要更强的设备内置隐私控制和更清晰的用户退出选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/11/258078_LG_G5_OLED_TV_JHiggins_0007.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C7.9509095732762%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/11/258078_LG_G5_OLED_TV_JHiggins_0007.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C7.9509095732762%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49639647' data-tags='Gaming,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49639647' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iZODN0QUgjI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IKEA made a mod for Skyrim [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宜家为《天际》制作的模组</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49639647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 575</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49639647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49639647' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IKEA released a Skyrim mod that brings real-world furniture into the game, letting players place recognizable pieces from the catalog in Tamriel. The accompanying video showcases how a furniture retailer can intersect with modding culture, hinting at new collaboration ideas between brands and community-driven games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宜家推出了一款《天际》模组，将真实世界的家具引入游戏，玩家可以在泰马里安的世界里摆放熟悉的家居。视频展示了品牌与玩家社区共建游戏内容的潜在协作路径，体现跨品牌跨界在游戏中的应用前景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746618' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49746618' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-2-27b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bonsai 2 27B: Near-Lossless Compression in a 9x Smaller Footprint</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bonsai 2 27B：9倍体积的近似无损压缩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746618' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new compression approach shrinks a large model’s footprint by about 9x while preserving near-lossless accuracy, enabling cheaper deployment on edge hardware. This work highlights the ongoing balance between model size and performance, with implications for on-device AI and inference cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项新的压缩方法在近似无损精度下将大型模型的体积约缩小9倍，使其能够在边缘设备上更低成本地部署。此类技术凸显了模型尺寸与性能之间的权衡，对边缘AI和推理成本具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/699604cc2b9dd89bdbda0608/6a6d0e0bc0d5f9a97a5164c0_69cc089669ab0df26e921aac_prism-og-img%2520(2).png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/699604cc2b9dd89bdbda0608/6a6d0e0bc0d5f9a97a5164c0_69cc089669ab0df26e921aac_prism-og-img%2520(2).png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49745940' data-tags='AI,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49745940' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/astra-for-law/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astra for Law</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astra 在法律领域的应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49745940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49745940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 677</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49745940' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astra for Law extends the Astra platform into legal work, offering AI-powered tooling for research, drafting, and analysis. It promises faster due diligence and more consistent drafting while raising concerns about accuracy, bias, and the need for expert oversight in regulated domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astra 将其平台扩展到法律工作，提供AI驱动的研究、起草和分析工具。它可能提升尽职调查速度与文书的一致性，但在受监管领域也引发对准确性、偏见和专业监督需求的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5nKwJBPqWZBjTJ2BWxguU/96ed72fb0f3b5f4c468e1a1009bbf30f/introducing-astra-for-law-seo-image.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5nKwJBPqWZBjTJ2BWxguU/96ed72fb0f3b5f4c468e1a1009bbf30f/introducing-astra-for-law-seo-image.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49892245' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49892245' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/delhi-electricity-loss' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Delhi cut electricity loss from 50 to 5 percent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德里如何将电力损耗从50%降至5%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49892245' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delhi dramatically reduced electricity distribution losses from about 50% to around 5% through targeted reforms, grid upgrades, and better metering. The move showcases how smart infrastructure and governance can unlock huge efficiency gains for urban utilities and could serve as a scalable model for other cities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德里通过有针对性的改革、输配电网升级和改进计量，将电力损耗从约50%降至约5%左右。此举展示了智慧基础设施与治理在提升城市电力效率方面的潜力，并具有可复制性供其他城市参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67793961&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C250%2C0%2C250' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67793961&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C250%2C0%2C250' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49570772' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49570772' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.gallup.com/poll/713933/record-high-say-government-corruption-widespread.aspx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Record-High 89% in U.S. Say Government Corruption Widespread</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国创纪录的89%认为政府腐败普遍存在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 528</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49570772' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gallup reports a record 89% of Americans see government corruption as widespread, signaling deep distrust in institutions. For technologists, this sentiment shapes policy considerations around transparency, regulation, and the social context of digital infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>盖洛普调查显示有创纪录的 89% 的美国人认为政府腐败普遍存在，反映了对机构的深度不信任。对技术政策与数字治理而言，这一情绪会影响透明度、监管与公共基础设施的信任建设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imagekit.gallup.com/fusion/tr:n-emd_soc_meta_og/POLL/85e4dc00-82a3-45cb-b881-981ea10dfa90.jpg' data-full='https://imagekit.gallup.com/fusion/tr:n-emd_soc_meta_og/POLL/85e4dc00-82a3-45cb-b881-981ea10dfa90.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49883844' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49883844' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/firelex/jeff' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jeff – Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models, trained at home, ~30 ms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jeff：Jev 兼容的0.8B决策模型，在家训练，推理约30毫秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49883844' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jeff introduces Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models that can be trained at home and run inference in about 30 ms. The lightweight scale enables on-device experimentation, lowering barriers for individuals and small teams to prototype decision systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jeff 提供 Jev 兼容的 0.8B 决策模型，可在本地训练，推理时延约 30 毫秒。此举降低了个人和小团队在本地进行 AI 原型开发的门槛，凸显对隐私和本地化计算友好的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58007a3c610cd76359a2607408649873459234efe21b764d80c3b27618864eb2/firelex/jeff' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58007a3c610cd76359a2607408649873459234efe21b764d80c3b27618864eb2/firelex/jeff' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49507822' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49507822' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openshot.org/blog/2026/08/30/openshot-40-record-edit-color-like-never-before/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenShot 4.0 – Open-source video editor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenShot 4.0：开源视频编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49507822' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenShot 4.0 introduces major UI and performance improvements, with enhanced recording, editing, and color tools. The update strengthens OpenShot as a beginner-friendly, cross-platform editor and demonstrates sustained, community-driven development in open-source video tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenShot 4.0 在界面与性能方面带来显著改进，新增录制、编辑和色彩工具。此次更新巩固了它作为面向初学者、跨平台的编辑器的定位，同时体现了开源视频工具领域的社区驱动持续发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.openshot.org/images/uploads/zinnia/2026/08/30/color-view.webp' data-full='https://cdn.openshot.org/images/uploads/zinnia/2026/08/30/color-view.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49624394' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49624394' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/culture/infinite-scroll/flock-wants-a-closely-surveilled-world-with-no-exit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock Wants a Closely Surveilled World with No Exit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 想要一个紧密监控、无出口的世界</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 542</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49624394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A New Yorker feature critiques surveillance-centric tech ecosystems and argues that Flock envisions a world where tracking is ubiquitous and inescapable. It ties corporate data practices to broader social costs, including autonomy and democratic agency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新 Yorker 的专栏批评以监控为核心的技术生态，认为 Flock 想象的是一个无处不在、难以逃脱的监控世界。文章将企业数据实践与自主权和民主治理的代价联系起来，强调对隐私的深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6aa05e7b6979461f46da3deb/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/NewYorker_flockcamera_final.jpg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6aa05e7b6979461f46da3deb/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/NewYorker_flockcamera_final.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49832938' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49832938' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.substack.com/p/liver' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is the liver so weirdly regenerative?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么肝脏的再生能力如此奇特？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49832938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 563</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49832938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49832938' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An explainer on why the liver regenerates so well, covering biology, stem cells, and signaling pathways, with implications for regenerative medicine and therapy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释肝脏为何具备如此强的再生能力，涉及生物学、干细胞与信号通路，并对再生医学与治疗具有潜在启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49604870' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49604870' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://weirdgloop.org/blog/google-jail' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There&#x27;s a new &quot;Google Jail&quot; for independent wikis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>独立维基的新型“Google Jail”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49604870' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Independent wikis are facing new restrictions or demotion in Google search, dubbed a &#x27;Google Jail&#x27; scenario.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立维基在谷歌搜索中的可见性遭遇新的限制，被形容为‘Google Jail’。文章分析搜索索引变动如何影响社区成长与曝光，以及小型项目需要如何适应平台的排名机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://weirdgloop.org/images/Weird%20Gloop%20square%20logo.png' data-full='https://weirdgloop.org/images/Weird%20Gloop%20square%20logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49681152' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49681152' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jetkvm.com/blog/introducing-jetkvm-mini' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JetKVM Mini</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JetKVM Mini</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49681152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49681152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49681152' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JetKVM expands its KVM-over-IP lineup with the JetKVM Mini, a compact, budget-friendly model designed for remote server management. The device promises the core KVM features in a smaller form factor, appealing to hobbyists and small teams who need remote control without bulky gear.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JetKVM 推出 JetKVM Mini，紧凑且更实惠的 KVM-over-IP 设备，面向需要远程服务器管理的个人和小团队。小巧机身保留核心功能，提供便捷的远程控制能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jetkvm.com/blog/jetkvm-mini/og-v2.jpg' data-full='https://jetkvm.com/blog/jetkvm-mini/og-v2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49673098' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49673098' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/interactive/briefing/2026/09/03/nvidia-is-the-central-bank-of-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia is the central bank of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达是AI的央行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49673098' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nvidia’s dominance in AI compute gives it outsized influence over model training, pricing, and access. As demand for accelerators grows, the company effectively sets the monetary policy of AI, shaping which startups succeed and which research directions are viable. This centrality invites regulatory and competitive scrutiny with broad ripple effects across ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达在AI计算力上的垄断地位，让其对模型训练、定价与准入拥有举足轻重的影响力。随着对加速器的需求攀升，这家公司实质上在制定AI的货币政策，左右哪些初创企业能生存、哪些研究方向可行。其高度集中性也引发对市场竞争和供应链的关注与潜在风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49523754' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49523754' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitlab.com/AuroraOSS/AuroraStore/-/work_items/1566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Play Store blocks AuroraStore, hurting GrapheneOS users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Play 商店封锁 AuroraStore，GrapheneOS 用户受影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49523754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 557</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49523754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49523754' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google blocks Aurora Store, limiting access for GrapheneOS users who rely on Aurora as an alternative to Google Play Services. This move heightens tensions between privacy-focused Android forks and Google&#x27;s control of app distribution. It underscores ongoing battles over who gets to decide what runs on Android devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 封禁 Aurora Store，影响依赖 Aurora 作为 Google 服务替代的 GrapheneOS 用户。此举加剧了隐私导向的 Android 派系与 Google 对应用分发控制之间的博弈。也凸显了对 Android 生态由谁来决定可运行软件的持续争议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/uploads/-/system/project/avatar/6922885/icon.png?v=1788283454' data-full='https://gitlab.com/uploads/-/system/project/avatar/6922885/icon.png?v=1788283454' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49521973' data-tags='Privacy,Web,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49521973' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mozilla.org/en/firefox/ad-blocker-on-ios/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introducing Ad Blocker for Firefox on iOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 iOS 的 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49521973' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox on iOS gains an integrated ad blocker, expanding privacy controls on Apple&#x27;s platform. This moves more browser vendors toward built-in content filtering, improving speed and battery life for readers. It demonstrates ongoing improvements to iOS web tooling that bypasses some mobile ad economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 在 iOS 版 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能，增强对广告与跟踪的控制，改善隐私与浏览体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/08/iOS-AdBlocker-1-1080x720.png' data-full='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/08/iOS-AdBlocker-1-1080x720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49896050' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49896050' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/09/draftkings-using-ai-supercharge-harms-online-behavioral-advertising' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DraftKings is using AI to behaviorally target chronic gamblers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DraftKings 正在用 AI 针对慢性赌徒进行行为定向</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49896050' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending. This raises urgent ethical and regulatory concerns about predatory targeting, calling for stronger safeguards, transparency, and oversight to protect vulnerable users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。这一做法引发关于掠夺性定向、伦理与监管的紧迫关切，需加强保护措施、透明度和监管以保护脆弱用户。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/mobile-privacy.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/mobile-privacy.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49726586' data-tags='Security,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49726586' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/hackers-flock-camera-data-shows-how-system-works/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers Got Inside a Flock Camera</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客入侵 Flock 摄像头，其数据揭示系统运作方式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49726586' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers breached a Flock camera, and the retrieved data reveals how the system authenticates, communicates with the cloud, and handles footage. The leak provides a rare inside look at the telemetry and control signals powering modern surveillance hardware, highlighting potential weaknesses in device-to-cloud architectures. It underscores the need for stronger firmware integrity and better security practices in IoT surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客入侵了 Flock 摄像头，窃取的数据揭示了该系统如何进行认证、与云端通信以及如何处理影像。此举让人得以窥见现代监控硬件背后的遥测与控制信号，揭示设备与云端架构中的潜在弱点。它强调需要加强固件完整性和物联网监控的安全实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6aa9a89861c11f99a88f271a/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Hackers%20Got%20Inside%20a%20Flock%20Camera.%20Its%20Data%20Shows%20How%20the%20System%20Really%20Works.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6aa9a89861c11f99a88f271a/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Hackers%20Got%20Inside%20a%20Flock%20Camera.%20Its%20Data%20Shows%20How%20the%20System%20Really%20Works.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49778029' data-tags='Hardware,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49778029' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.sedaily.com/finance/2026/09/20/samsung-to-double-hbm4-output-next-year-sources-say' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung is expected to more than double output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星HBM4/HBM4E DRAM产量有望翻倍以上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49778029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49778029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 453</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49778029' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung is reportedly set to more than double the output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM next year to meet surging AI and data-center demand. The planned capacity ramp could help ease tight supply for high-bandwidth memory used in GPUs and accelerators, potentially impacting pricing and supply chains across hyperscalers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三星计划在来年把 HBM4 与 HBM4E DRAM 的产量大幅提升，超过现有水平的两倍，以满足 AI 与数据中心对高带宽内存的需求。这一扩产将缓解相关供需紧张，可能影响 GPU/加速器市场的定价与供应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wimg.sedaily.com/news/cms/2026/09/20/rcv.YNA.20260318.PYH2026031803840001300_P1.jpg' data-full='https://wimg.sedaily.com/news/cms/2026/09/20/rcv.YNA.20260318.PYH2026031803840001300_P1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49857899' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49857899' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://themomoftheyear.substack.com/p/im-the-mom-in-that-viral-giants-clip' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m the mom in that viral Giants clip. Let me tell you about my husband</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我是那段病毒视频里的妈妈，谈谈我的丈夫</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49857899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 550</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49857899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49857899' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From the mom behind a viral Giants clip, she offers context about her husband and what the moment meant for their family.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自那个在网络走红的巨人队视频背后的妈妈，讲述她丈夫以及这次曝光给家庭带来的影响。此文揭示了网红时刻如何改变普通人的生活，并促使读者重新审视广为传播的视频背后故事。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49732270' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49732270' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mimo.xiaomi.com/rl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xiaomi Mimo 2.6 live post-training dashboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小米 Mimo 2.6：实时后训练仪表板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49732270' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xiaomi Mimo 2.6 introduces a live post-training dashboard that gives engineers real-time visibility into model performance after training runs. It enhances experiment tracking, metrics comparison, and configuration debugging across iterations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>小米 Mimo 2.6 引入实时的后训练仪表板，为工程师提供训练完成后的模型性能实时可视化。它增强了实验跟踪、指标对比和跨迭代的配置调试能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49561320' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49561320' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.techdirt.com/2026/09/03/hackers-had-a-live-feed-of-every-id-this-verification-company-scanned-for-over-a-year/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers had a live feed of every ID verification company scanned for over a year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流持续一年多</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49561320' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reveal that attackers maintained a live stream of every ID verification company&#x27;s scanned data for more than a year, exposing systemic privacy and security risks in identity verification ecosystems. The breach underscores how third-party verification pipelines can leak sensitive data and why rigorous monitoring and supply-chain controls matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流保持了超过一年的持续访问，暴露身份验证生态中的隐私和安全风险。事件凸显了第三方验证链路的脆弱性，以及加强监控与供应链控制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='http://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49776699' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49776699' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pirateface.co/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pirate Face Rescues LLM Models from Deletion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pirate Face 拯救被删除的语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49776699' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pirate Face promotes archiving and rehosting LLMs that are at risk of removal, arguing for preservation of research artifacts and open access. The project highlights a broader tension between platform takedowns and community-maintained backups, with implications for licensing, provenance, and long-tail AI research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pirate Face 旨在存档并重新托管可能被删除的语言模型，主张保存研究成果并促进开放获取。该项目凸显了平台下架与社区维护备份之间的张力，对许可、来源和长期 AI 研究具有深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pirateface.co/og/home' data-full='https://pirateface.co/og/home' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
