<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/09/m2-episode-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux on M3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux 在 M3 平台上的进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 337</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49586698' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux shows progress on Apple M3, bringing Linux support to a newer generation of Apple silicon. Early milestones suggest progress toward a usable Linux experience on M3 machines. The effort demonstrates the ongoing, rapid pace of Apple Silicon support within the Linux community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 在 Apple M3 芯片上显示出进展，朝着在新一代 Apple SoC 上实现 Linux 支持迈进。早期里程碑表明有望在 M3 设备上实现可用的 Linux 体验，体现了 Linux 社区对 Apple Silicon 的持续快速适配。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pointinthecloud.com/2026-04-11-211700.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fixing the Portobello Police Station Clock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>修复波托贝洛警察局的时钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49817469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49817469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49817469' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An on-site repair of a long-standing public clock at Portobello Police Station highlights the practical challenges of keeping public infrastructure accurate. The piece walks through diagnosis, component choices, and the care required to maintain legacy devices in a public setting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在波托贝洛警察局对长期使用的公开时钟进行现场修复，展示保持公共基础设施准时性的实际挑战。文章梳理诊断过程、部件选择以及维护老旧设备的要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pointinthecloud.com/media/rosie-flying-dog.jpg' data-full='https://pointinthecloud.com/media/rosie-flying-dog.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49877988' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49877988' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.alexewerlof.com/p/coding-is-not-solved' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Coding is not solved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编码问题尚未解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49877988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49877988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 521</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49877988' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging. AI can help but cannot replace the craft of software engineering or the need for disciplined practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。AI 能帮助，但无法取代软件工程的工艺性与规范性实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-Pk4!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F409bf75a-d9ef-4adf-b242-bee9d2664a24_1280x1247.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-Pk4!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F409bf75a-d9ef-4adf-b242-bee9d2664a24_1280x1247.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49881747' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49881747' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://definitelynotwindows.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11½</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11½（幽默笔记）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49881747' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A humorous take on the idea of Windows 11.5, lampooning release cadence, feature promises, and the never-ending iterations of Windows. It riffs on user frustration and the gap between hype and usability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以对 Windows 11.5 的讽刺笔记为载体，调侃版本迭代节奏、功能承诺与实际使用之间的错位，反映用户对系统变更的情绪与现实体验的落差。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://definitelynotwindows.com/social-preview.png' data-full='https://definitelynotwindows.com/social-preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49840054' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49840054' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aymannadeem.com/artificial/intelligence,/developer/tools/2026/09/24/plan-mode-is-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plan mode is dead</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计划模式已死</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49840054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49840054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 452</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49840054' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that explicit &#x27;plan mode&#x27; for AI agents is no longer necessary; modern agents can effectively operate via tool integration and dynamic prompting without a separate planning step. The shift reduces overhead but requires robust tool ecosystems and guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 AI 代理中的显式“计划模式”已不再必要；通过工具集成和动态提示，代理可以在没有独立规划阶段的情况下工作。此转变降低了开销，但需要更健全的工具生态和监管机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.aymannadeem.com/assets/images/plan-mode-is-dead/code-generation-vs-interfaces.png' data-full='https://www.aymannadeem.com/assets/images/plan-mode-is-dead/code-generation-vs-interfaces.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49772961' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/spain-blocks-archive-today-and-mirrors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain orders blocks on Archive.today and its mirrors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙下令封锁 Archive.today 及其镜像站点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49772961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49772961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 409</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49772961' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain has ordered blocks on Archive.today and its mirrors, impacting access to archived web content. The move raises questions about digital preservation, freedom of information, and enforcement in a modern information landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙下令封锁 Archive.today 及其镜像站点，影响对已存档网页内容的访问。这一举措引发关于数字保存、信息自由与在现代信息环境中的执法等问题的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/images/2026/09/VlEW9CtTFnlS.jpg' data-full='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/images/2026/09/VlEW9CtTFnlS.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49721322' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49721322' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://security.apple.com/blog/apple-reference-image/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Reference Image: A New Approach for Verified Photography</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果参考图像：用于认证摄影的新方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49721322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49721322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 346</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49721322' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils the Reference Image approach to verify photography provenance on devices, enabling cryptographic-style checks and image-forensics workflows. The goal is to harden trust in photos, making spoofed or manipulated imagery easier to detect at capture or processing time. This strategy could reshape how apps and services verify media authenticity in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出参考图像，用以验证摄影的来源与真实性。通过对比参考信息，系统可更早检测伪造或篡改的照片，提升数字影像的可信度。此方法可能改变媒体真实性的验证标准与流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://security.apple.com/assets/image/seo-image.jpg' data-full='https://security.apple.com/assets/image/seo-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49578866' data-tags='Health,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49578866' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://feld.com/archives/2026/09/the-real-luxuries-in-life/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Real Luxuries In Life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人生中真正的奢侈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49578866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49578866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49578866' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Real luxuries aren&#x27;t gadgets or wealth. Time, health, and meaningful relationships are what truly matter, and the piece urges founders to protect these assets as they build.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>真正的奢侈不是金钱或物质，而是时间、健康和意义深厚的人际关系。文章劝创业者在追求成就的同时，优先保护这些最宝贵的资产。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://feld.com/og-default.png' data-full='https://feld.com/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49610631' data-tags='Programming,Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49610631' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ayghri/i-have-adhd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I-have-ADHD: A skill to stop coding agents from burying the answer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我有 ADHD：阻止编码代理把答案埋起来的技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 352</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49610631' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal take on managing ADHD while building AI agents, sharing practical tactics like task structuring, explicit goals, and timeboxing to keep solutions on track.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者结合 ADHD 的经验，给出在构建 AI 代理时保持聚焦的实用方法，如任务结构化、明确目标与时间盒管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/dbd81be60f3edfa52cc24cf94d183c35fe8ac6e6cef7dd16a16c21de5653cb56/ayghri/i-have-adhd' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/dbd81be60f3edfa52cc24cf94d183c35fe8ac6e6cef7dd16a16c21de5653cb56/ayghri/i-have-adhd' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49710253' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49710253' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c8ly49w9g1edo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Suspected sabotage causes major Netherlands rail disruption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰铁路重大中断疑似蓄意破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 455</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49710253' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A major rail disruption in the Netherlands appears to be caused by suspected sabotage, as authorities investigate the motive and method. The incident underscores how critical rail networks remain vulnerable to targeted disruption and how operators balance rapid service restoration with security in signaling and infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰出现大规模铁路中断，调查初步指向疑似蓄意破坏。此事件凸显铁路网络对有针对性的破坏仍然脆弱，并在修复与安全之间带来新的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/eda4/live/dbe5e120-b0de-11f1-b1d1-571ed4d7ff2c.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/eda4/live/dbe5e120-b0de-11f1-b1d1-571ed4d7ff2c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49757520' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49757520' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/09/18/politics/us-military-ai-false-intelligence-china-ship' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Military had close call after using AI for hallucinated intelligence report</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国军方因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些行动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 509</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49757520' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. military nearly acted on an AI-generated intelligence report that turned out to be hallucinated; this incident underscores the danger of trusting AI outputs in high-stakes operations and the importance of human vetting. Officials are recalibrating procedures, with emphasis on stronger verification and safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美军因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些据此行动，凸显高风险场景下对AI输出进行人工核验的重要性。相关方正加强验证流程、人机协同与安全措施来降低此类风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/9884637.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/9884637.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49630253' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/09/apple-introduces-airpods-5-with-best-in-class-open-ear-active-noise-cancellation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AirPods 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AirPods 5：全球最佳开耳式主动降噪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 507</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49630253' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple announces AirPods 5 with best-in-class open-ear active noise cancellation and upgraded mics for clearer calls. The open-ear design aims to balance situational awareness with quieter audio, signaling continued innovation in wearable audio ergonomics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出 AirPods 5，全球最佳的开耳式主动降噪与升级麦克风提升通话清晰度。开耳佩戴设计在提升环境感知的同时提供更安静的聆听体验，体现可穿戴音频领域的持续创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/09/apple-introduces-airpods-5-with-best-in-class-open-ear-active-noise-cancellation/tile/Apple-AirPods-5-hero-260909-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202609091909' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/09/apple-introduces-airpods-5-with-best-in-class-open-ear-active-noise-cancellation/tile/Apple-AirPods-5-hero-260909-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202609091909' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49719249' data-tags='Data,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49719249' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/world/middle-east/aws-says-it-cant-restore-some-data-from-mideast-facilities-struck-by-iran-ddcb7e5d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS says it can&#x27;t restore some data from mideast facilities struck by Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS：中东设施受袭后部分数据无法恢复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49719249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 503</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49719249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 422</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49719249' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS says some data cannot be restored after Iran-struck Middle East facilities, underscoring limits of disaster recovery and the value of cross-region backups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS 表示在被伊朗袭击的中东设施中，部分数据无法恢复，凸显灾备的局限性，以及跨区域备份的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49604637' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49604637' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mcpherrin.ca/2026/09/07/rsa.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;ve factored the RSA keys of a Certificate Authority from the 90s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我已经分解了90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 503</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49604637' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Factoring a 1990s-era CA RSA key underscores how legacy cryptography remains a risk. The disclosure prompts re-issuance of certificates under stronger keys, revocation considerations, and a push toward modern algorithms and shorter key lifetimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥进行分解，揭示了旧式加密的长期风险。此事可能推动重新颁发证书、吊销受影响的证书，并促使向更强的算法和更短的密钥寿命过渡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mcpherrin.ca/assets/rsa/filtered.png' data-full='https://mcpherrin.ca/assets/rsa/filtered.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49905487' data-tags='Politics,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49905487' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomwojcik.com/posts/2026-09-21/september-2026-the-world-today/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>September 2026: The world today, as seen by one Polish guy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026年9月：一个波兰人眼中的世界</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49905487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49905487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 379</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49905487' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From a Polish vantage point, the post surveys how September 2026 reshapes tech policy, AI adoption, and global power dynamics. It blends on-the-ground observations with macro signals about EU regulation and startup funding. The takeaway is a cautious view of where innovation and geopolitics intersect.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从波兰视角出发，文章梳理了2026年9月全球科技政策、AI采用与地缘政治格局的变化。结合实地观察与宏观信号，分析欧盟监管与初创资金的趋势。核心在于警觉地看待创新与地缘政治如何交汇。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tomwojcik.com/og/september-2026-the-world-today.jpg' data-full='https://tomwojcik.com/og/september-2026-the-world-today.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49784336' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49784336' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://consumerrights.wiki/w/Disney%2B_ad_policy_change' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Disney+: New user agreement allows ads before movies in all subscriptions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Disney+：新用户协议将允许在所有订阅中影片前插播广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49784336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 501</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49784336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49784336' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disney+ is updating its user agreement to allow ads before movies across all subscription tiers, signaling a broader push toward ad-supported streaming. The change could affect user experience and retention while enabling higher ad-driven revenue for the service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Disney+ 将在所有订阅层级允许在电影放映前投放广告，标志着向广告支持型流媒体的扩张。此举可能影响用户体验和留存，也为 Disney 带来更高的每账户收入潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://consumerrights.wiki/images/logo/new_fixed_logo.png' data-full='https://consumerrights.wiki/images/logo/new_fixed_logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49891480' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49891480' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/sep/29/trial-live-facial-recognition-cameras-london-stations-false-positive' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>500k facial scans at UK stations yield no arrests, 1 false positive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国车站的五十万次人脸识别未逮捕，仅一例误报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 501</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49891480' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large-scale facial recognition test at UK rail stations produced no arrests and just one false positive from half a million scans. The result highlights limited predictive value for crime prevention and rekindles debates over privacy and surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国铁路站点的大规模人脸识别测试在累计约50万次扫描中未逮捕任何人，仅有一次误报。结果凸显对治安预防的实际效用有限，再次引发对隐私与监控的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/59a72cd2826be7e1e72f407ba3a515ec43697227/546_44_4210_3370/master/4210.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0c92b09e04de7a9aa71a1822d47b9589' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/59a72cd2826be7e1e72f407ba3a515ec43697227/546_44_4210_3370/master/4210.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0c92b09e04de7a9aa71a1822d47b9589' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662672' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49662672' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://capitalbnews.org/data-centers-permit-rules-epa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The EPA is planning to scrap public review rules for data center pollution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EPA 正在评估取消数据中心污染公开审查规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 500</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49662672' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EPA is weighing changes that would ease or remove public comment requirements on data center pollution permits. Critics warn that reduced transparency could shift environmental risk away from communities, while supporters argue streamlined permitting accelerates infrastructure. The development signals tension between rapid data-center growth and watchdog oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>环保署正在评估可能放宽或取消数据中心污染许可的公众审查规则。批评者警告，透明度下降会将环境风险从社区转移，而支持者则认为简化许可有助于基础设施建设。这一动向凸显数据中心快速扩张与监督之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/capitalbnews.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GettyImages-2266751110-scaled.jpg?fit=2560%2C1707&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/capitalbnews.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GettyImages-2266751110-scaled.jpg?fit=2560%2C1707&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49695876' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49695876' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tenderlovemaking.com/2026/09/11/what-a-time-to-be-alive/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI bots knew about the RubyGems caching vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 系统早知 RubyGems 缓存漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49695876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 498</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49695876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49695876' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues OpenAI systems had awareness of a RubyGems caching vulnerability, illustrating how AI tools can absorb and potentially propagate software supply chain risks. The takeaway is that AI deployments must account for latent security knowledge and policy implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中主张 OpenAI 的系统对 RubyGems 的缓存漏洞有提前认知，揭示 AI 工具可能吸收并传播软件供应链风险。对 AI 部署而言，这强调需要将潜在安全知识与政策影响纳入考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tenderlovemaking.com/images/logo1.png' data-full='https://tenderlovemaking.com/images/logo1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49780630' data-tags='Politics,Business,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49780630' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://truthout.org/articles/warren-introduces-bill-to-ban-private-equity-from-owning-medical-practices/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bill to Ban Private Equity from Owning Medical Practices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拟禁私募股权控股医疗机构的法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 497</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 369</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49780630' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bill would bar private equity firms from owning medical practices, aiming to curb cost pressures and preserve care quality. If enacted, the policy could reshape healthcare investment, affect consolidation trends, and influence how health-tech startups partner with providers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拟议法案禁止私募股权控股医疗机构，旨在遏制成本压力并维护护理质量。若通过，或将重塑医疗投资格局，影响诊所整合趋势，并改变健康科技公司与提供者的合作模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://truthout.org/app/uploads/2026/09/2026_09-17-elizabeth-warren.jpg' data-full='https://truthout.org/app/uploads/2026/09/2026_09-17-elizabeth-warren.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49597907' aria-label='Show summary and image'>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49597907' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It offers practical tips for unplugging and recharging, with relevance to developers and knowledge workers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文描述了一种以大脑休整和抵御信息过载为目的的假期方式，并提供了实用的 unplugging 策略。内容对开发者和知识工作者具有现实意义，帮助在高强度工作中重新充电。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49467636' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49467636' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rockpapershotgun.com/dwarf-fortress-is-getting-the-mother-of-all-magic-updates-extending-to-the-fundamental-cosmological-makeup-of-the-universe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dwarf Fortress is getting the mother of all magic updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《矮人要塞》将迎来史上最强大魔法更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49467636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 497</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49467636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49467636' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dwarf Fortress is getting a massive magic-focused update that may overhaul spell systems and cosmology, expanding how players manipulate the game world. The release could redefine modding and endgame strategies for one of gaming&#x27;s most intricate sims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《矮人要塞》将迎来规模空前的魔法更新，预计重塑咒语系统和宇宙观，改变玩家对世界的操控方式。此举或将重新定义模组创作与游戏后期策略，巩固其作为极度复杂模拟游戏的地位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assetsio.gnwcdn.com/Dwarf-Fortress_l6U4yxY.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale&amp;auto=webp' data-full='https://assetsio.gnwcdn.com/Dwarf-Fortress_l6U4yxY.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale&amp;auto=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49726007' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49726007' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wax_motor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wax motor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜡动机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 494</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49726007' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A wax motor uses the expansion of paraffin wax when heated to produce motion, offering quiet operation and low maintenance. It is commonly used in thermostats and valve control, providing a simple, durable actuation method with trade-offs in response time and temperature sensitivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蜡动机通过加热使石蜡膨胀来产生运动，工作安静、维护成本低，广泛用于恒温器和阀门控制等场景。其简单耐用的驱动方式虽成本低，但受温度和响应时间限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/4e/ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg/1280px-ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' data-full='https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/4e/ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg/1280px-ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49627370' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49627370' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/gpt-6-astra-looped-transformers-and' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-6 Astra, looped transformers, and hidden reasoning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-6 Astra、循环化 Transformer 与隐藏推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49627370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 492</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49627370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49627370' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra explores looping Transformer architectures and the possibility of hidden reasoning in large language models. It highlights implications for scalability, interpretability, and safety as capabilities advance, and underscores the need for careful evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra 探索循环化 Transformer 架构以及大型语言模型中的隐藏推理可能性。讨论了这一设计对可扩展性、可解释性和安全性的影响，并强调在能力提升阶段需要更严格的评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!bGKc!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F3d2ac2ec-ab80-43a8-aa93-a33a846dda43_1564x874.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!bGKc!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F3d2ac2ec-ab80-43a8-aa93-a33a846dda43_1564x874.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49828731' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49828731' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://macanorak.com/two-tier-encryption-in-the-uk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two-tier encryption in the UK</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国两层加密：同一设备不同保护水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49828731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 492</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49828731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 453</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49828731' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UK policy reportedly creates two tiers of encryption, meaning identical devices might face different protection levels depending on context. The analysis weighs privacy and security trade-offs, potential backdoors, and what this means for users, manufacturers, and end-to-end encryption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国两层加密政策意味着相同设备在不同情境下可能受到不同的保护。文章分析了隐私与安全的折中、潜在的后门风险，以及此举对用户和制造商的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://macanorak.com/content/images/2026/09/Two-Tier-Encrypyion-Cover-Image.png' data-full='https://macanorak.com/content/images/2026/09/Two-Tier-Encrypyion-Cover-Image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49536375' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49536375' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trellner.com/reports/manufactured-sources-behind-ai-recommendations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three sites made 215,128 “best software” pages for AI. Perplexity cites them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三家站点为 AI 制作了 215,128 个“最佳软件”页面，Perplexity 引用它们</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 490</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49536375' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three sites collectively produced 215,128 pages labeled &#x27;best software&#x27; used by AI systems, according to a Trellner report cited by Perplexity. The finding raises concerns about data provenance, the quality of training sources, and how such manufactured sources may shape AI recommendations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三家站点合计制作了 215,128 个标注为“最佳软件”的页面，被 AI 系统使用，据 Trellner 的报告被 Perplexity 引用。此发现引发对数据来源、训练源质量的担忧，以及制造性来源如何影响 AI 推荐的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://trellner.com/og.png' data-full='https://trellner.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49569896' data-tags='Privacy,DevOps,Web,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49569896' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.statichost.eu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Statichost.eu – European static site hosting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Statichost.eu：欧洲静态网站托管服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49569896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 489</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49569896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49569896' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Statichost.eu offers Europe-based static site hosting with privacy-focused features and fast delivery. By leveraging Git-based workflows and data locality, it serves as a privacy-conscious alternative to US-centric hosts for developers hosting personal sites or documentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Statichost.eu 提供位于欧洲的静态站点托管，强调隐私与快速交付，并支持基于 Git 的部署工作流。对于希望数据在地理上留在欧洲、并注重治理与隐私的开发者来说，这是 US 主流托管的可选替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.statichost.eu/%20preview.png' data-full='https://www.statichost.eu/%20preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49842764' data-tags='Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49842764' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/InfinityLoop1308/PipePipe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PipePipe: NewPipe hard fork implementing SponsorBlock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PipePipe：NewPipe 的硬分叉，实现 SponsorBlock</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49842764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49842764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49842764' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PipePipe is a hard fork of NewPipe that integrates SponsorBlock directly, enabling automatic skipping of sponsor segments in videos. This move concentrates user-side ad-skipping controls in an open-source mobile YouTube client, with potential effects on monetization and creator funding models. It also shows how forked open-source projects can quickly adopt niche features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PipePipe 是对 NewPipe 的硬分叉，内置 SponsorBlock 功能，能够自动跳过视频中的赞助段落。此举将广告跳过控制权带入开源移动端应用，可能影响创作者的收益与平台生态，同时体现开源社区对定制功能的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f756384b56b6d20313b6dad868d5de1ac3ad8241a6733fe107541f40206951b3/InfinityLoop1308/PipePipe' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f756384b56b6d20313b6dad868d5de1ac3ad8241a6733fe107541f40206951b3/InfinityLoop1308/PipePipe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49508982' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49508982' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.macrumors.com/2026/08/30/apple-unexpected-mac-mini-and-studio-demand/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple caught off guard by AI demand for Mac Mini and Mac Studio</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果被 AI 需求冲击 Mac Mini 与 Mac Studio</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49508982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49508982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 579</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49508982' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple reportedly faces unexpected demand for AI workloads pushing Mac Mini and Mac Studio beyond forecasted capacity, prompting questions about hardware supply and Apple&#x27;s AI strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称 AI 需求意外拉升对 Mac Mini 与 Mac Studio 的需求，超过了公司预期，暴露出硬件供应与 AI 策略之间的紧张关系。文章讨论了消费级硬件在 AI 场景中的应用及对供应与定价的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.macrumors.com/t/OsRlBLMFhQf-DlTIBvCnu2Opj54=/2500x/article-new/2026/04/Mac-mini-vs-Studio-Feature-Sans-Text-1.jpg' data-full='https://images.macrumors.com/t/OsRlBLMFhQf-DlTIBvCnu2Opj54=/2500x/article-new/2026/04/Mac-mini-vs-Studio-Feature-Sans-Text-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49824268' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49824268' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.meta.com/vr-glasses/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta VR Glasses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 的 VR 眼镜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49824268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49824268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49824268' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta unveils a new line of VR glasses designed to complement the Quest ecosystem with lighter, more portable form factors. The move signals a broader push toward AR-like experiences and could broaden the hardware and software opportunities for developers and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 正推出新款 VR 眼镜，旨在让 Quest 生态更轻便、便携化，并模糊虚拟现实与增强现实的边界。这一举措凸显其在硬件形态进化上的策略，或为开发者带来更多机会并提升用户吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=1061526633457028&amp;version=1790036507&amp;transcode_extension=webp' data-full='https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=1061526633457028&amp;version=1790036507&amp;transcode_extension=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49527396' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49527396' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Sep/1/codex-libreoffice/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The ChatGPT/Codex app bundles a full copy of LibreOffice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT/Codex 应用捆绑 LibreOffice 的完整副本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49527396' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A ChatGPT/Codex-based app ships LibreOffice in full, raising questions about dependency bloat and licensing. It also touches on maintenance and update responsibilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 ChatGPT/Codex 的应用捆绑了 LibreOffice 的完整副本，引发对依赖膨胀、许可合规与分发责任的关注。文章分析将大型开源套件嵌入 AI 工具的潜在风险，以及后续的维护与更新责任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/codex-primay-runtime.webp' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/codex-primay-runtime.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49700477' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49700477' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andonlabs.com/blog/why-we-built-pion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pion, an agent designed to run any company autonomously</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pion：一个可让企业自主运行的自治代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 601</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49700477' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pion is an agent designed to run any company autonomously, orchestrating tasks and decisions with minimal human input. This concept highlights the potential and challenges of deployable autonomous organizations, including governance, safety, and reliability considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pion 是一个旨在让企业在最小人工干预下自行运作的自治代理。该理念展示了可落地的全自动化组织前景，同时也提出治理、安全与可靠性方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andonlabs.com/pion.og.webp' data-full='https://andonlabs.com/pion.og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49715947' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49715947' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-3-8-live-gemini-3-8-live-extended-thinking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 3.8 Live and 3.8 Live Extended Thinking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.8 Live 与 3.8 Live Extended Thinking</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715947' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google releases Gemini 3.8 Live and its Extended Thinking variant, boosting reasoning, multi-step problem solving, and tool usage in real time. The update underscores advances in AI reasoning capabilities and how Google is balancing speed, safety, and utility in consumer-facing models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 推出 Gemini 3.8 Live 与 3.8 Live Extended Thinking，显著提升模型的推理、分步问题解决和工具调用能力。更新体现了在提升推理水平的同时，如何在速度、安全性与实用性之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini_3-8_live___keyword__blog-social.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini_3-8_live___keyword__blog-social.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49845977' data-tags='Legal,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49845977' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/09/25/pentagon-anthropic-ai-risk-appeals-court.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. appeals court upholds designation of Anthropic as supply chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国上诉法院维持对Anthropic的供应链风险认定</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49845977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 481</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49845977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 843</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49845977' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal appeals court confirms government contractors must treat Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, reinforcing risk-management and vendor due-diligence requirements. For AI developers and buyers, that feeds into compliance burdens, eligibility for sensitive work, and potential shifts in who can bid on government AI projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国上诉法院维持对Anthropic的供应链风险认定，要求在政府采购中加强对该供应商的风险评估。对AI公司和政府买方意味着增加合规负担、影响参与敏感项目的资格，以及对政府合同的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108285631-1774986195722-gettyimages-2268688342-raa-anthropi260331_npMR4.jpeg?v=1774986237&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108285631-1774986195722-gettyimages-2268688342-raa-anthropi260331_npMR4.jpeg?v=1774986237&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
